\phi_{2}(t)=f(t)+h_{2}(t)>f(t)+a=f(t)+\sup_{s\in[0,t^{*}_{2}]}\{(-f(s))^{+}\}\geq f(t)+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\{(-f(s))^{+}\}\geq 0.

However, since
h_{2}(t^{*}_{2})>a\geq h_{2}(t_{1}^{*})
we have
\int_{(t^{*}_{1},t^{*}_{2}]}dh_{2}(t)>0
, which, since
\phi_{2}(t)>0
for all
t\in(t^{*}_{1},t^{*}_{2}]
contradicts the fact that
\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{I}_{\{\phi_{2}(t)>0\}}dh_{2}(t)=0
. Thus we have a contradiction and so we must have
h_{2}(t)\leq h_{1}(t)
for all
t\in[0,T]
which implies
\phi_{2}(t)\leq\phi_{1}(t)
for all
t\in[0,T]
.

Now we will prove that
\phi_{1}\leq\phi_{3}
. It is sufficient to show that
h_{3}\geq h_{1}
. Once again arguing via contradiction, assume there exists
t^{*}_{2}\in[0,T]
such that
h_{3}(t^{*}_{2})<h_{1}(t^{*}_{2})=\sup_{s\in[0,t^{*}_{2}]}\{(-f(s))^{+}\}
. Then there exists
t^{*}_{1}\in[0,t^{*}_{2}]
such that
(-f(t^{*}_{1}))^{+}>h_{3}(t^{*}_{2})\geq h_{3}(t^{*}_{1})
which implies
(-f(t^{*}_{1}))^{+}=f(t^{*}_{1}
and
\phi_{3}(t^{*}_{1})=f(t^{*}_{1})+h_{3}(t^{*}_{1})<f(t^{*}_{1})-f(t^{*}_{1})
meaning
\phi_{3}(t^{*}_{1})<0
. However this contradicts the fact that
\phi_{3}\geq 0
which proves that
h_{3}\geq h_{1}
.
∎

We now proceed to the proof of Proposition
3.2
.
Fix
t\geq 0
. Define

\hat{I}^{r}_{i}(s)\doteq\int_{(t,t+s]}\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(u)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}>0\}}d\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(u),\;s\geq 0.

Then, from (
3
), for
s,t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}

\displaystyle=\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)

\displaystyle\quad+\hat{I}^{r}_{i}(s)+\int_{(t,t+s]}\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(u)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\leq 0\}}d\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(u)

and consequently from the first part of Proposition
5.1
we have

\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\leq\Gamma_{1}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{I}^{r}_{i}(\cdot)\right)(s)

for all
s\in[0,T]
. In addition,

\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+s)=\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(t)

where
\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(t)
is nondecreasing and nonnegative, and
\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)\geq 0
so using the second part of Proposition
5.1
once more, we have

\Gamma_{1}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)\right)(s)\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+s)

(12)

for all
s\in[0,T]
. Since

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\Big|\Gamma_{1}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)\right)(s)

\displaystyle\quad-\Gamma_{1}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{I}^{r}_{i}(\cdot)\right)(s)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\Big|\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{I}^{r}_{i}(s)\right)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq 2Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+2\int_{(t,t+T]}\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(u)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}>0\}}d\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(u)

we have, for all
s\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\Gamma_{1}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)\right)(s)

\displaystyle\geq\Gamma_{1}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{I}^{r}_{i}(\cdot)\right)(s)-2Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}

\displaystyle\quad-2\int_{(t,t+T]}\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(u)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}>0\}}d\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(u)

\displaystyle\geq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+s)-3Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}-2\int_{(t,t+T]}\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(u)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}>0\}}d\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(u).

(13)

Combining (
12
) and (
13
), we have,

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\left\{|\Gamma_{1}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)+\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t+\cdot)-\hat{X}^{r}_{i}(t)\right)(s)-\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+s)|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 3Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+2\int_{(t,t+T]}\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(u)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}>0\}}d\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(u).

In addition due to Proposition
2.6
part (d) we have

\displaystyle\int_{(t,t+T]}\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(u)-Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}>0\}}d\hat{U}^{r}_{i}(u)\leq r^{-1}C_{i}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}(t+T)}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds.

The result follows.
∎

6
Proof of Proposition
3.3

We will use the following two propositions in the proof of Proposition
3.3
. The proof of the first proposition is a simpler version of that of Proposition
7.5
which gives a similar estimate for service times. Since the proof of the latter result is given in full detail in
Section
9.1
, we omit the proof of Proposition
6.1
. We make the convention that
u_{j}^{r}(0)=0
for
r\in\mathbb{N}
and
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
.

Proposition 6.1
.

Let
\delta
be as in Condition
1
. There exists
R<\infty
and for any
c<\delta
a corresponding
K(c)<\infty
such that for any
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
,
y^{r}\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
, and
t\geq 0

we have

\sup_{r\geq R}E\left[e^{cu^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t))}\right]<K(c).

The proof of the next proposition is in Section
9.2
.

Proposition 6.2

(Proof in Section
9.2
)
.

Let
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
,
c_{1},c_{2}\geq 0
, and
\epsilon>0
be arbitrary. Then there exists
B_{1},B_{2},R\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
T\in[0,\infty)
and
r\geq R
we have

P\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq r^{2c_{1}+c_{2}}T}|A_{j}^{r}(t)-t\alpha^{r}_{j}|\geq\epsilon r^{c_{1}+c_{2}}T\right)\leq B_{1}e^{-r^{c_{2}}TB_{2}}

(14)

and

P\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq r^{2c_{1}+c_{2}}\max_{i}\{C_{i}\}T}|S_{j}^{r}(t)-t\beta^{r}_{j}|\geq\epsilon r^{c_{1}+c_{2}}T\right)\leq B_{1}e^{-r^{c_{2}}TB_{2}}.

(15)

In particular, for
\kappa\geq 0
,

P\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}T}|A_{j}^{r}(t)-t\alpha^{r}_{j}|\geq\epsilon r^{\kappa}T\right)\leq B_{1}e^{-Tr^{\frac{1}{2}\kappa}B_{2}}.

(16)

and

P\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\max_{i}\{C_{i}\}T}|S_{j}^{r}(t)-t\beta^{r}_{j}|\geq\epsilon r^{\kappa}T\right)\leq B_{1}e^{-Tr^{\frac{1}{2}\kappa}B_{2}}.

(17)

We note that equations (
16
) and (
17
) are immediate from (
14
) and
(
15
) on taking
c_{1}=c_{2}=\kappa/2
.
The former set of equations are essential to understanding the behavior of
Q^{r}_{j}(t)
in the region
[0,\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}]
in the proof of Proposition
3.3
; see for example the proof of (
28
) completed below (
30
), and the estimates in (
34
) and (
38
).

We now proceed to the proof of Proposition
3.3
.

Overall Proof idea.
Recall, as described in Definition
2.3
, that
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(t)
changes from
0
to
1
when
Q^{r}_{j}(t)
drops below
\tilde{c}_{1}r^{\kappa}
. This says that we stop processing type
j
jobs until
Q^{r}_{j}(t)
reaches
\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}
at which point
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(t)
changes back to
0
and the processing of type
j
jobs resumes. The key to this proof is that if
Q^{r}_{j}(t)<\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}
then type
j
jobs are processed at a rate lower than their arrival rate (see Proposition
2.6
part (e)) in an attempt to boost
Q^{r}_{j}(t)
back up to
\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}
. To prove this result we first get an upper bound on
P(\mathcal{C}^{r})
where
\mathcal{C}^{r}
, defined in (
19
), is the event that
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}t)
fails to exceed
\tilde{c}_{1}r^{\kappa}-1
by time
t=\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+Tr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}
. Note that due to Proposition
2.6
part (a) once
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}t)
enters the region
[\tilde{c}_{1}r^{\kappa}-1,\infty)
it never leaves, and it is convenient for the remainder of this proof to focus on the event
(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
where this entrance has occurred by time
t=\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+Tr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}
. The bulk of the proof is devoted to providing an upper bound on

P\left(\int_{r\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+Tr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}}^{r^{2}T}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(t)=1\}}dt\geq\epsilon Tr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa}\right).

This is accomplished by dividing the time interval
[\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+Tr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2},T]
into subintervals of length
r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}
and showing that bad behavior on the
n
th subinterval, as defined by the set
\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r}
in (
20
), is unlikely. On the set
(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
we can use the frequency of the events
\{\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r}\}_{n\geq 1}
to bound the amount of time the process is in the state
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(t)=1
using (
27
). Demonstrating that with high probability
\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r}
only occurs for a small percentage of subintervals completes the proof.

Let
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
be arbitrary. Let
R_{1}<\infty
and
\Delta>0
be such that for all
r\geq R_{1}
we have

2<r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16},\;r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{\Delta 4}<r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa},\mbox{ and if }Q^{r}_{j}(t)<\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa},\mbox{ then }\beta^{r}_{j}\mathfrak{b}^{r}_{j}(t)\leq\alpha^{r}_{j}-\Delta.

(18)

Existence of such a
R_{1}
and
\Delta
follows from
Proposition
2.6
part (e).

For
n\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
define

\hat{t}_{n}=\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+Tr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}+(n-1)r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}
.
In addition, for
n\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
define the sets

\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}=\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\geq r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\right\},\;\;\;\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}=\left\{\tau^{r,S}_{j}\left(\hat{t}_{n}\right)<\tau^{r,S}_{j}\left(\hat{t}_{n+1}\right)\right\},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\{\sup_{\bar{\xi}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\leq s\leq\bar{\xi}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})+r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})}\Big|A^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)-A^{r}_{j}(r^{2}\bar{\xi}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))

\displaystyle\kern 5.0pt-r^{2}(s-\bar{\xi}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))\alpha^{r}_{j}\Big|\quad>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\{\sup_{0\leq s\leq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})}\Big|A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}s)-r^{2}s\alpha^{r}_{j}\Big|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\{\sup_{\bar{\xi}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\leq s\leq\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n+1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})+\bar{\xi}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})}\Big|S^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)-S^{r}_{j}(r^{2}\bar{\xi}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))

\displaystyle\kern 5.0pt-r^{2}(s-\bar{\xi}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))\beta^{r}_{j}\Big|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\{\sup_{0\leq s\leq\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n+1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})}\Big|S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}s)-r^{2}s\beta^{r}_{j}\Big|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\Big\}.

Also define

\mathcal{C}^{r}=\{Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{1})<r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}-1\}.

(19)

From (
1
), we have, for all
s\geq 0
and
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}

\displaystyle Q^{r}(r^{2}s)=

\displaystyle q^{r}_{j}+A^{r}_{j}(r^{2}(s-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j})^{+})+\mathcal{I}_{\{s\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}>0\}}

\displaystyle-S^{r}_{j}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}\right)^{+}\right)-\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}>0\}}

and for any
n\geq 1
and
\hat{t}_{n}\leq s\leq\hat{t}_{n+1}
we have

\displaystyle A^{r}_{j}(r^{2}(s-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j})^{+})+\mathcal{I}_{\{s\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}>0\}}=

\displaystyle A^{r}_{j}(r^{2}(\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j})^{+})+\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{t}_{n}\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}>0\}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{I}_{\{s-\hat{t}_{n}\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})>0\}}+A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}(s-\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+})

and

\displaystyle S^{r}_{j}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}\right)^{+}\right)+\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}>0\}}

\displaystyle=S^{r}_{j}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}\right)^{+}\right)+\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}>0\}}+\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})>0\}}

\displaystyle\quad+S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\right)^{+}\right).

Let

\zeta^{r,n}_{0}\doteq\inf\{s\geq\hat{t}_{n}:Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}\}

and for
l\geq 0
define

\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}\doteq\inf\{s\geq\zeta^{r,n}_{2l}:Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)<r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}\},\;\;\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+2}\doteq\inf\{s\geq\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}:Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}\}.

If
\zeta^{r,n}_{0}>\hat{t}_{n}
then for all
s\in[\hat{t}_{n},\zeta^{r,n}_{0})
we have
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)<r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}
. Consequently for all
s\in[\hat{t}_{n},\zeta^{r,n}_{0})
we have
\beta^{r}_{j}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\right)\leq\left(s-\hat{t}_{n}\right)\left(\alpha^{r}_{j}-\Delta\right)
. In addition, on
(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
,
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}-1
for all
s\geq\hat{t}_{1}
because if
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)<r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}
then
\tilde{\mathcal{E}}^{r}_{j}(s)=1
so
\frac{d}{ds}\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)=0
due to Proposition
2.6
part (a). Consequently, with

\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r}\doteq\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}\cup\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}\cup(\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}),

(20)

on the set
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
for all
s\in[\hat{t}_{n},\zeta^{r,n}_{0})\cap[\hat{t}_{n},\hat{t}_{n+1}]
we have

\displaystyle Q^{r}(r^{2}s)

\displaystyle=Q^{r}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{n})+\mathcal{I}_{\{s-\hat{t}_{n}\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})>0\}}-\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})>0\}}

\displaystyle\quad+A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}(s-\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+})-S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+})

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}-2+r^{2}\alpha^{r}_{j}(s-\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+}-r^{2}\beta^{r}_{j}(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+}

\displaystyle\quad-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{8}

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}-2+r^{2}\alpha^{r}_{j}\left(s-\hat{t}_{n}\right)-r^{2}\alpha^{r}_{j}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-r^{2}\beta^{r}_{j}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\right)

\displaystyle\quad-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{8}

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}+r^{2}\alpha^{r}_{j}\left(s-\hat{t}_{n}\right)-\alpha^{r}_{j}\left(r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16\alpha^{r}_{j}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-r^{2}\beta^{r}_{j}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\right)-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{8}

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{4}+\Delta r^{2}\left(s-\hat{t}_{n}\right)

(21)

where the last two lines use (
18
).
Recall that
\Delta>0
and from (
18
)
r^{2}(\hat{t}_{n+1}-\hat{t}_{n})>r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4\Delta}
so if
\zeta^{r,n}_{0}-\hat{t}_{n}>r^{\kappa-2}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4\Delta}
then, on
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
,

Q^{r}\left(r^{2}\left(\hat{t}_{n}+r^{\kappa-2}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4\Delta}\right)\right)\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{4}+\Delta r^{2}\left(r^{\kappa-2}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4\Delta}\right)=r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}

which contradicts the definition of
\zeta^{r,n}_{0}
so on the set
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
we have

\zeta^{r,n}_{0}-\hat{t}_{n}\leq r^{\kappa-2}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4\Delta}
and consequently
\zeta^{r,n}_{0}<\hat{t}_{n+1}
. Therefore on the set
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
we have

r^{2}\left(\zeta^{r,n}_{0}-\hat{t}_{n}\right)\leq r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4}.

(22)

By definition, for all
l\geq 0
,
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}
for all
s\in[\zeta^{r,n}_{2l},\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}]
so
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}s)=0
for all
s\in[\zeta^{r,n}_{2l},\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1})
. On the set
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
for any
l\geq 0
satisfying
\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}<\hat{t}_{n+1}
and
s\in[\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1},\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+2})\cap[\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1},\hat{t}_{n+1}]
we have

\displaystyle Q^{r}(r^{2}s)

\displaystyle=Q^{r}(r^{2}\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1})+\mathcal{I}_{\{s-\hat{t}_{n}\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})>\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}-\hat{t}_{n}\}}-\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})>\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}(s-\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+})-A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}(\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}-\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+})\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\left(S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+})\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+})\right)

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}-2-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{8}+r^{2}\alpha^{r}_{j}\left((s-\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+}-(\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}-\hat{t}_{n}-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{8}-r^{2}\beta^{r}_{j}\left((\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))^{+}\right)

since
Q^{r}_{j}\left(r^{2}\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}\right)\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}-1
.
Consequently

\displaystyle Q^{r}(r^{2}s)

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}-r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{16}+r^{2}\alpha^{r}_{j}\left(s-\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}\right)-r^{2}\alpha^{r}_{j}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})-r^{2}\beta^{r}_{j}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1})\right)

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{2}-r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{16}+r^{2}\Delta\left(s-\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}\right)-\alpha^{r}_{j}\left(r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\right)

\displaystyle\geq r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right)+r^{2}\Delta\left(s-\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}\right),

where once more we have used (
18
).
Because
\Delta>0
we have
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)\geq r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right)
for all
s\in[\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1},\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+2}\wedge\hat{t}_{n+1}]
and because by definition
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1})=0
we have
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}s)=0
for all
s\in[\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1},\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+2})\cap[\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1},\hat{t}_{n+1}]
. Since
l\geq 0
such that
\zeta^{r,n}_{2l+1}<\hat{t}_{n+1}
was arbitrary we have
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}s)=0
for all
s\in[\zeta^{r,n}_{0},\hat{t}_{n+1}]
on the set
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
. In addition, we have shown that on the set
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
we have
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)\geq r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right)
for all
s\in[\zeta^{r,n}_{0},\hat{t}_{n+1}]
so
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1})\geq r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right)
. Consequently, from (
22
), on the set
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
we have

\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds=\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\zeta^{r,n}_{0}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds+\int_{r^{2}\zeta^{r,n}_{0}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\leq r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4},

(23)

\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1})=0,\mbox{ and, }Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1})\geq r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right).

(24)

In particular, with
H^{r}_{n}=\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{n})=0\}\cap\left\{Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{n})\geq r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right)\right\}
, for
n>2
we have

(H^{r}_{n})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{U}_{n-1}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}=\emptyset

(25)

Next, on the set
H^{r}_{n}\cap(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
for

s\in[\hat{t}_{n},\zeta^{r,n}_{0})
(recall that
\zeta^{r,n}_{0}<\hat{t}_{n+1}
on
(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
) we have, from similar calculations as in (
21
)

\displaystyle Q^{r}(r^{2}s)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right)+r^{2}\Delta\left(s-\hat{t}_{n}\right)-\alpha^{r}_{j}\left(r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\right)-r^{\kappa}\frac{3(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{16}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle r^{\kappa}\left(\frac{3}{8}\tilde{c}_{2}+\frac{5}{8}\tilde{c}_{1}\right)+\Delta r^{2}\left(s-\hat{t}_{n}\right).

Because
\Delta>0
this implies
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)>r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}
for all

s\in[\hat{t}_{n},\zeta^{r,n}_{0})
and since
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}\hat{t}_{n})=0
this implies
\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}s)=0
for all
s\in[\hat{t}_{n},\zeta^{r,n}_{0})
. Consequently on
H^{r}_{n}\cap(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}\cap(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
we have that (
24
) holds and

\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds=\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\zeta^{r,n}_{0}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds+\int_{r^{2}\zeta^{r,n}_{0}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds=0,

(26)

Therefore for any
N\geq 1
we have, on
(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
,

\displaystyle\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{1}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{N}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}}\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds+\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{H^{r}_{n}\cap(\mathcal{U}_{n}^{r})^{c}}\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{(H^{r}_{n})^{c}\cap\left(\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}\right)^{c}}\int_{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n}}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{n+1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}}+r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4}\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{(H^{r}_{n})^{c}\cap\left(\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}\right)^{c}},

where we have used (
24
) and (
26
) in obtaining the last inequality.

From (
25
) we have, on
(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
,

\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{(H^{r}_{n})^{c}\cap\left(\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}\right)^{c}}\leq 1+\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}}.

Therefore, on
(\mathcal{C}^{r})^{c}
,

\int_{0}^{r^{2}\hat{t}_{N}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\leq r\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+Tr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}+r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4}+\left(r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}+r^{\kappa}\frac{5(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{4}\right)\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}}.

(27)

From the above estimate, in order to prove the result, it now suffices to show that there exists
R,B<\infty
such that for all
r\geq R
and
T\geq 1
, we have

P(\mathcal{C}^{r})\leq e^{-BTr^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}},

(28)

and

P\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{U}^{r}_{n}}\geq 3r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T\right)\leq e^{-BTr^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}}.

(29)

For (
28
), note that since from Proposition
2.6
part (a), if
Q^{r}_{j}(r^{2}s)<r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}
then
\frac{d}{ds}\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)=0
, we have that

\displaystyle P(\mathcal{C}^{r})\leq P(A^{r}_{j}(Tr^{3\kappa/2})\leq r^{\kappa}\tilde{c}_{1}).

(30)

The estimate in (
28
) now follows readily from (
16
).

The estimate in (
29
) follows if we can show that there exists
R,B<\infty
such that for all
r\geq R
and
T\geq 1
we have

P\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}\right)}\geq r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T\right)\leq e^{-BTr^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}},\\

(31)

P\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}}\geq r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T\right)\leq e^{-BTr^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}},\\

(32)

and

P\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}\right)^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}}\geq r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T\right)\leq e^{-BTr^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}}.\\

(33)

First we show (
31
), with
B=\frac{1}{4}
, for
r
sufficiently large.
Define

\mathcal{F}^{r,S}_{j}(k)=\sigma\left\{u^{r}_{l}(m^{u}_{l}),v^{r}_{l^{\prime}}(m^{v}_{l^{\prime}}),v^{r}_{j}(m^{v}_{j}):m^{v}_{l^{\prime}}\geq 0,m^{u}_{l}\geq 0,l\in\mathbb{A}_{J},l^{\prime}\in\mathbb{A}_{J}\setminus\{j\},m^{v}_{j}\leq k\right\}

which is the filtration that contains the information on all inter-arrival times, all service times from queues other than the
j
-th queue, and the first
k
service times from queue
j
.
Note that
\tau^{r,S}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})
is a
\mathcal{F}^{r,S}_{j}(k)
stopping time and thus with
\mathcal{H}^{j}_{n}\doteq\mathcal{F}^{r,S}_{j}(\tau^{r,S}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))
for
n\geq 0
,
\{\mathcal{H}^{j}_{n}\}_{n\geq 0}
is a filtration. Note that for
k<n

\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{k}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{k}
is
\mathcal{H}^{j}_{n}
-measurable. In addition,
\bar{\xi}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})
is
\mathcal{H}^{j}_{n}
-measurable
and
S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}
is independent of
\mathcal{H}^{j}_{n}
. Write

\mathcal{U}_{n}=\exp\{\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}}\}=e^{1/2}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}}+\mathcal{I}_{(\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n})^{c}}.

Then we have

E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}}}\right]=E\left[\prod_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{U}_{n}\right]=E\left[E(\mathcal{U}_{N}\mid\mathcal{H}^{j}_{N-1})\prod_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{U}_{n}\right]\\
\leq E\left[\left[\left(1-P\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{N}|\mathcal{H}^{j}_{N-1}\right)\right)+e^{\frac{1}{2}}P\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{N}|\mathcal{H}^{j}_{N-1}\right)\right]\prod_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{U}_{n}\right].\\

For
n\geq 0
, since
\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\geq 0
and
\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n+1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\leq\max_{i}\{C_{i}\}r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}
, we have

P\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}|\mathcal{H}^{j}_{n}\right)\leq P\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq\max_{i}\{C_{i}\}r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}}\left|S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}s)-r^{2}s\beta^{r}_{j}\right|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\right)

and due to Proposition
6.2
(
17
) there exists
R_{2}\in[R_{1},\infty)
and
B_{1}<\infty
such that for all
r\geq R_{2}
and
n\geq 0
we have

P\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq\max_{i}\{C_{i}\}r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}}\right|S^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}s)-r^{2}s\alpha^{r}_{j}\left|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\right)\leq e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}.

(34)

Since
\left(1-p+pe^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)
is an increasing function of
p
we have

E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}}}\right]\leq E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}}}\right]\left(1+e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}}-1\right)\right).

Now by a standard recursive argument we see

E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}}}\right]\leq\left(1+e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}\left(e^{1/2}-1\right)\right)^{N+1}.

Since
e^{\frac{1}{2}}-1\leq 1
we have

1+e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}}-1\right)\leq e^{e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}}

which gives

E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}}}\right]\leq e^{(N+1)e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}}.

(35)

Choose
R_{3}\in[R_{2},\infty)
such that for all
r\geq R_{3}
and
T\geq 1
we have

-\frac{1}{2}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T+\left(Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}+1\right)e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}\leq-\frac{1}{4}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T.

Then for all
r\geq R_{3}
and
T\geq 1
we have from (
35
) that

\displaystyle P\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,1}_{n}\cap\mathcal{B}^{r,2}_{n}\right)}\geq r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{1}{2}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T+\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil e^{-r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}B_{1}}}\leq e^{-\frac{1}{4}Tr^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}}.

This completes the proof of (
31
).
Let
\delta>0
be as in Condition
1

and let
\upsilon\in(0,\delta)
be arbitrary. Now
we will show that (
32
) holds with
B=\frac{\upsilon}{2}
for
r
sufficiently large.
For
x\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
define

\tilde{\tau}^{r,A}_{j}(x)=\min\left\{k\geq 0:\sum_{l=1}^{k}u^{r}_{j}(l)\geq x\right\},

Let
\check{u}^{r}_{j}(x)\doteq\mathcal{I}_{\{x>0\}}u^{r}_{j}\left(\tilde{\tau}^{r,A}_{j}(x)\right)
and define

\Phi^{r}_{\upsilon}(x)\doteq E\exp\left\{\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\left\{\check{u}^{r}_{j}(x)>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\right\}}+\upsilon\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{I}_{\left\{\check{u}^{r}_{j}(x)>lr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}\right\}.

Let

\mathcal{F}^{r,A}_{j}(k)=\sigma\left\{u^{r}_{l^{\prime}}(m^{u}_{l^{\prime}}),v^{r}_{l}(m^{v}_{l}),u^{r}_{j}(m^{u}_{j}):m^{u}_{l^{\prime}}\geq 0,m^{v}_{l}\geq 0,l\in\mathbb{A}_{J},l^{\prime}\in\mathbb{A}_{J}\setminus\{j\},m^{u}_{j}\leq k\right\}

which is the filtration that contains the information on all all service times, all inter-arrival times for queues other than the
j
-th queue, and the first
k
arrival times from queue
j
. Note that
\tau^{r,A}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})
is a
\mathcal{F}^{r,A}_{j}(k)
-stopping time and thus with
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{j}_{n}\doteq\mathcal{F}^{r,A}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n}))
for
n\geq 0
,
\{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{j}_{n}\}_{n\geq 0}
is a filtration.
For
n\geq 0
and a
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{j}_{n}
-measurable positive random variable
X
define

\tilde{\tau}^{r,A,n}_{j}(X)=\min\left\{k\geq 0:\sum_{l=\tau^{r,A}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})+1}^{l=\tau^{r,A}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})+k}u^{r}_{j}(l)\geq X\right\}.

and

\displaystyle\psi^{r,n}_{\upsilon}(X)

\displaystyle\doteq

\displaystyle\exp\left[\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\left\{X>0,u^{r}_{j}\left(\tilde{\tau}^{r,A}_{j}(X)+\tau^{r,A}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\right)>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\right\}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\left.+\upsilon\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{I}_{\left\{X>0,u^{r}_{j}\left(\tilde{\tau}^{r,A}_{j}(X)+\tau^{r,A}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\right)>lr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}\right].

Note that

E\left[\psi^{r,n}_{\upsilon}(X)|\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{j}_{n}\right]=\Phi^{r}_{\upsilon}(X).

(36)

Due to Proposition
6.1
there exists
R_{4}\in[R_{3},\infty)
and
B_{2}<\infty
such that

\sup_{r\geq R_{4},x\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}E\left[e^{\upsilon\check{u}^{r}_{j}(x)}\right]\leq B_{2}

and for all
r\geq R_{4}
we have
e^{-\upsilon\left(r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}-1\right)}<\frac{1}{2}
,
r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\leq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}
,
2\alpha^{r}_{j}\geq\alpha_{j}
, and
r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}32}>1
.
Consequently for all
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
l\geq 1
we have

P\left(\check{u}^{r}_{j}(x)\geq r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\right)\leq B_{2}e^{-\upsilon r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}},\;\;P\left(\check{u}^{r}_{j}(x)\geq lr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right)\leq B_{2}e^{-\upsilon lr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}}.

Therefore for
r\geq R_{4}
we have

\displaystyle\Phi^{r}_{\upsilon}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+e^{\upsilon}B_{2}e^{-\upsilon r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}}+\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}e^{\upsilon(l+1)}B_{2}e^{-\upsilon lr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+e^{\upsilon}B_{2}e^{-\upsilon r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}}+e^{2\upsilon}B_{2}e^{-\upsilon r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}}\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}e^{-\upsilon l\left(r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}-1\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+e^{\upsilon}B_{2}e^{-\upsilon r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}}+2e^{2\upsilon}B_{2}e^{-\upsilon r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+3B_{2}e^{2\upsilon}e^{-\upsilon r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}}\leq e^{3B_{2}e^{2\upsilon}e^{-\upsilon r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}}}.

Letting
B_{3}=3B_{2}e^{2\upsilon}
and
B_{4}=\upsilon\frac{2(\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1})}{\alpha_{j}16}
we have that, for
r\geq R_{4}
,

\sup_{r\geq R_{4},x\in\mathbb{R}}\left\{\Phi^{r}_{\upsilon}(x)\right\}\leq e^{B_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}}

which combined with equation (
36
) implies that for any
n\geq 0
and
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{j}_{n}
-measurable real valued random variable
X
we have a.e.

E\left[\psi^{r,n}_{\upsilon}(X)|\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{j}_{n}\right]=\Phi^{r}_{\upsilon}(X)\leq e^{B_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}}.

(37)

Next,

\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{N}}\leq\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})\geq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}+\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})<r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{N}}.

Also,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})<r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{N}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}-r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})>0,u^{r}_{j}\left(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})+\tilde{\tau}^{r,A,N-1}_{j}\left(r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}-r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})\right)\right)>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{\alpha^{r}_{j}16}\right\}}.

Thus

\displaystyle e^{\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{N}}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})\geq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}+\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})<r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{N}}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})\geq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}}\psi^{r,N-1}_{\upsilon}(r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}-r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})).

By conditioning, using (
37
), and the fact that
r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}-r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})
is
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{j}_{N-1}
-measurable it then follows

\displaystyle E\left[e^{\upsilon\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}}}\right]\leq e^{B_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}}Ee^{\upsilon\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}}+\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{N-1})\geq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}}.

By a successive conditioning argument we now have that

Ee^{\upsilon\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}}}\leq e^{NB_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}}Ee^{\upsilon\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{0}}+\upsilon\sum_{l=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\left\{r^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{0})\geq lr^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\right\}}}.

By definition
\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{0})=0
so the expectation on the right side equals

1

which gives

E\left[e^{\upsilon\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}}}\right]\leq e^{NB_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}}.

Choose
R_{5}\in[R_{4},\infty)
such that for all
r\geq R_{5}
and
T\geq 1
we have

-T\upsilon r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}+Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}B_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}\leq-T\frac{\upsilon}{2}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}.

Then for all
r\geq R_{5}
and
T\geq 1
we have

\displaystyle P\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}}\geq r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T\right)\leq e^{-\upsilon r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T}E\left[e^{\upsilon\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\upsilon r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T+\left(\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1\right)B_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}}\leq e^{-\upsilon r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T+Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}B_{3}e^{-B_{4}r^{\kappa}}}\leq e^{-\frac{\upsilon}{2}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T}.

This proves (
32
).

Finally, we will show (
33
), with
B=1/4
and for
r
sufficiently large.
Note that for
n\geq 0
since
A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(\cdot)
is independent of
\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))
,
\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})
is
\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))
-measurable, and
\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(\hat{t}_{n})\geq 0
we have

\displaystyle P\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}|\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}}\left|A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}s)-r^{2}s\alpha^{r}_{j}\right|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}|\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}}\left|A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}s)-r^{2}s\alpha^{r}_{j}\right|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\right).

From Proposition
6.2
(
16
) there exists
R_{6}\in[R_{5},\infty)
and
B_{5}<\infty
such that for all
r\geq R_{6}
we have

P\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq r^{\frac{3}{2}\kappa-2}}\left|A^{r,\hat{t}_{n}}_{j}(r^{2}s)-r^{2}s\alpha^{r}_{j}\right|>r^{\kappa}\frac{\tilde{c}_{2}-\tilde{c}_{1}}{16}\right)\leq e^{-2B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}

(38)

and
e^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}
.
Therefore for all
r\geq R_{6}
and
n\geq 0
we have

P\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}|\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))\right)\leq e^{-2B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}

and so

\displaystyle E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{I}_{\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}\right)^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}}}|\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+e^{\frac{1}{2}}P\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}|\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+e^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-2B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\right)\leq\left(1+e^{-B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\right).

Since for
0\leq m<n
the set
\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{m}\right)^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{m}
is
\mathcal{F}^{r}(\tau^{r}(\hat{t}_{n}))
-measurable we have by a successive conditioning argument

\displaystyle E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{I}_{\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}\right)^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+e^{-B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\right)^{N}E\left[e^{\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{I}_{\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{0}\right)^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{0}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+e^{-B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\right)^{N}e^{1/2}\leq e^{Ne^{-B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}}e^{1/2}.

Choose
R_{7}\in[R_{6},\infty)
such that for all
r\geq R_{7}
and
T\geq 1
we have
-\frac{1}{2}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T+Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}e^{-B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}+\frac{1}{2}\leq-\frac{1}{4}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T
. Then for all
r\geq R_{7}
we have

\displaystyle P\Big(\sum_{n=1}^{\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1}\mathcal{I}_{\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,1}_{n}\right)^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}^{r,2}_{n}}\geq r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T\Big)

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{2}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T+\left(\lceil Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}\rceil-1\right)e^{-B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{2}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T+Tr^{2-\frac{3}{2}\kappa}e^{-B_{5}r^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}+\frac{1}{2}}\leq e^{-\frac{1}{4}r^{\frac{1}{8}\kappa}T}.

Consequently for all
r\geq R_{7}
we have that (
31
)-(
33
) hold which proves (
29
).
As noted previously, this completes the proof.
∎

7
Proof of Propositions
3.4

and
3.5

In this section we provide proofs of Propositions
3.4

and
3.5
.

7.1
Proof of Proposition
3.4

We begin with some preliminary stability results.

Definition 7.1
.

Let
\tilde{\upsilon}>0
and
\xi\geq 0
be arbitrary and define

\tilde{\gamma}^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i,\xi}\doteq\inf\left\{t\geq\xi:\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\hat{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)<\tilde{\upsilon}\right\}.

Proof of the following Proposition is in Section
8.1
.

Proposition 7.1

(Proof in Section
8.1
)
.

There exist constants
\tilde{\delta},{B}_{1},{B}_{2},{B}_{3},{R}\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
c\in(0,\tilde{\delta}]
,
r\geq{R}
,
\tilde{\upsilon}>0
,
\xi\geq 0
,

y^{r}=(\hat{q}^{r},\hat{\Upsilon}^{r},\tilde{\mathcal{E}}^{r})\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
, and
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
we have

E_{y^{r}}\left[e^{c\tilde{\gamma}^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i,\xi}}\right]\leq{B}_{1}e^{{B}_{2}\left(\xi+\tilde{\upsilon}+\hat{w}^{r}_{i}+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\hat{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}+\sum_{j=1}^{J}\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}\right)}+{B}_{3},

where
\hat{w}^{r}=KM^{r}\hat{q}^{r}
.

Definition 7.2
.

For
\tilde{\upsilon}>0
let
\tilde{\delta}
be as in Proposition
7.1
and for all
y=(\hat{q},\hat{\Upsilon},\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
, and
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
define

V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}\left(y\right)=E_{y}[e^{\tilde{\delta}\tilde{\gamma}^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i,0}}].

\{\hat{Y}^{r}(t)\}
from Section
2.5
.

Lemma 7.2

(Proof in Section
8.2
)
.

For
\tilde{\upsilon}>0
let
\tilde{\delta}
be as in Proposition
7.1
. There exist constants
B,R\in(0,\infty)
such that for any
y=(\hat{q},\hat{\Upsilon},\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
,
t\geq 0
,
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
, and
r\geq R
we have

E_{y}\left[V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}\left(\hat{Y}^{r}(t)\right)\right]\leq e^{-\tilde{\delta}t}V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}\left(y\right)+B.

Proposition 7.3

(Proof in Section
8.3
)
.

For any
\tilde{\upsilon}>0
there exist constants
B_{1},B_{2},R\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
y=(\hat{q},\hat{\Upsilon},\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
,
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
, and
r\geq R
we have, with
\hat{w}^{r}=KM^{r}\hat{q}
,

V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}(y)\geq B_{1}e^{B_{2}\left(\hat{w}^{r}_{i}-C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}\right)^{+}}.

We now proceed to the proof of Theorem
3.4
.

Fix
\tilde{\upsilon}>0
and note that from Proposition
7.3
there exist constants
B_{1},B_{2},R_{1}\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
y=(\hat{q},\hat{\Upsilon},\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
,
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
, and
r\geq R_{1}
we have

B_{1}e^{B_{2}\left(\hat{w}^{r}_{i}-C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}\right)^{+}}\leq V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}(y),

where
\hat{w}^{r}=KM^{r}\hat{q}
.
From Lemma
7.2
there exist constants
B_{3},\tilde{\delta}\in(0,\infty)
, and
R_{2}\in[R_{1},\infty)
such that for any
y=(\hat{q},\hat{\Upsilon},\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
,
t\geq 0
,
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
, and
r\geq R_{2}
we have

E_{y}\left[V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}\left(\hat{Y}^{r}(t)\right)\right]\leq e^{-\tilde{\delta}t}V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}\left(y\right)+B_{3}.

In addition, Proposition
7.1
implies that there exist constants
B_{4},B_{5},B_{6}\in(0,\infty)
and
R_{3}\in[R_{2},\infty)
such that for all
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
,
r\geq R_{3}
, and
y=(\hat{q},\hat{\Upsilon},\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
we have

V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}(y)\leq B_{4}e^{B_{5}(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+B_{6}.

Combining these three inequalities, we have, for all
t\geq 0

\displaystyle E_{y}\left[e^{B_{2}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}\right)^{+}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{B_{1}}E_{y}\left[V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}\left(\hat{Y}^{r}(t)\right)\right]

(39)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{e^{-\tilde{\delta}t}}{B_{1}}V^{r,\tilde{\upsilon}}_{i}\left(y\right)+\frac{B_{3}}{B_{1}}\leq\frac{B_{4}}{B_{1}}e^{-\tilde{\delta}t+B_{5}(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+\frac{B_{6}+B_{3}}{B_{1}}.

Next, from Proposition
6.1
, there exist constants
B_{7}\in(0,\infty)
and
R_{4}\in[R_{3}\vee 1,\infty)
, such that, with
\delta>0
as in Condition
1
, for all
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
, and
c\in(0,\delta)
we have

\sup_{r\geq R_{4},y\in\mathcal{Y}^{r},t\in[0,\infty)}E_{y}\left[e^{cu^{r}_{j}(\tau^{A,r}_{j}(t))}\right]\leq B_{7}.

Then for alll
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
,
t\geq 0
,
c\in(0,\delta)
, and
r\geq R_{4}
we have

\displaystyle E_{y}\left[e^{c\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)}\right]

\displaystyle=E_{y}\left[e^{c\mathcal{I}_{\left\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)=0\right\}}\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}+c\mathcal{I}_{\left\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0\right\}}\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)}\right]

(40)

\displaystyle\leq e^{c\mathcal{I}_{\left\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)=0\right\}}\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}}E_{y}\left[e^{\frac{c}{r}\mathcal{I}_{\left\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0\right\}}u^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t))}\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{c\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}}E_{y}\left[e^{c\mathcal{I}_{\left\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0\right\}}u^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t))}\right]\leq e^{c\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}}B_{7}.

Since
\left\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\right\}_{j=1}^{J}
are independent, for all
c\in(0,\delta)
,
t\geq 0
, and
r\geq R_{4}
we then have

\displaystyle E_{y}\left[e^{c\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}}\right]\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}E_{y}\left[e^{c\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)}\right]\leq e^{c\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}}JB_{7}.

(41)

Note that if
t>\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}
and
r\geq R_{4}\geq 1
then
\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0
for all
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
. Consequently, a similar estimate as for (
41
) shows that, for all
t>\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}
,
c\in(0,\delta)
, and
r\geq R_{4}
we have

\displaystyle E_{y}\left[e^{c\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}}\right]\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}E_{y}\left[e^{c\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)}\right]\leq JB_{7}.

(42)

Let

\delta_{1}\doteq\min\left\{B_{2}/2,\delta/(2C_{i})\right\}

and note that for all
c\in(0,\delta_{1})
,
t\geq 0
,
r\geq R_{4}
, and
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
we have

\displaystyle E_{y}\Big[e^{c\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq E_{y}\Big[\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)>2C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}\}}e^{2c\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{I}_{\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)\leq 2C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}\}}e^{2cC_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq E_{y}\left[e^{2c\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}\right)^{+}}\right]+E_{y}\left[e^{2cC_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq E_{y}\left[e^{B_{2}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)-C_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}\right)^{+}}\right]+E_{y}\left[e^{2cC_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{B_{4}}{B_{1}}e^{-\tilde{\delta}t+B_{5}(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+\frac{B_{6}+B_{3}}{B_{1}}+E_{y}\left[e^{2cC_{i}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\}}\right]

where the last line used equation (
39
).
Combining this with equations (
41
) and (
42
) implies that for any
c\in(0,\delta_{1})
,
r\geq R_{4}
, and
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
if
t\in[0,\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}]
we have

\displaystyle E_{y}\Big[e^{c\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)}\Big]\leq\frac{B_{4}}{B_{1}}e^{-\tilde{\delta}t+B_{5}(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+\frac{B_{6}+B_{3}}{B_{1}}+e^{\delta_{1}\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}}JB_{7}

and if
t>\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}
we have

\displaystyle E_{y}\Big[e^{c\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)}\Big]\leq\frac{B_{4}}{B_{1}}e^{-\tilde{\delta}t+B_{5}(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+\frac{B_{6}+B_{3}}{B_{1}}+JB_{7}.

Note that since
|\hat{\Upsilon}|\geq\max_{j}\{\hat{\Upsilon}^{A}_{j}\}
this implies that for any
c\in(0,\delta_{1})
,
r\geq R_{4}
,
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
, and
t\geq 0
we have

\displaystyle E_{y}\Big[e^{c\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)}\Big]\leq\left(\frac{B_{4}}{B_{1}}+JB_{7}\right)e^{-\tilde{\delta}t+(B_{5}+\delta_{1}+\tilde{\delta})(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+\frac{B_{6}+B_{3}}{B_{1}}+JB_{7}.

Define

\delta_{2}\doteq I^{-\frac{1}{2}}\delta_{1}

and note that for all
c\in(0,\delta_{2})
,
t\geq 0
, and
r\geq R_{4}
we have

\displaystyle E_{y}\left[e^{c|\hat{W}^{r}(t)|_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq E_{y}\left[e^{cI^{\frac{1}{2}}\max_{i}\{\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)\}}\right]\leq\sum_{i=1}^{I}E_{y}\left[e^{cI^{\frac{1}{2}}\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{I}\left(\left(\frac{B_{4}}{B_{1}}+JB_{7}\right)e^{-\tilde{\delta}t+(B_{5}+\delta_{1}+\tilde{\delta})(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+\frac{B_{6}+B_{3}}{B_{1}}+JB_{7}\right)

\displaystyle\leq I\left(\frac{B_{4}}{B_{1}}+JB_{7}\right)e^{-\tilde{\delta}t+(B_{5}+\delta_{1}+\tilde{\delta})(|\hat{q}|+|\hat{\Upsilon}|)}+I\left(\frac{B_{6}+B_{3}}{B_{1}}+JB_{7}\right).

Since
y\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
was arbitrary this completes the proof.
∎

7.2
Proof of Proposition
3.5

We begin with some auxiliary results.

Lemma 7.4
.

For all
s\geq 0
if
\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s)\not\in\mathcal{M}
then
\tilde{d}\left(\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\right)\vee 0\right)=0
.

Proof.

Let
s\geq 0
be arbitrary and assume
\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s)\not\in\mathcal{M}
. Note that for all
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
we have

\left(\hat{Q}_{j}^{r}(s)-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\right)^{+}=0

if
\mathcal{Z}^{r}_{j}(s)=1
. Consequently the result follows on applying Proposition
2.2

with
q=\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\right)^{+}
and noting that
z^{q}=\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s)
.
∎

The next result, which is analogous to Proposition
6.1
, is proved in Section
9.1
. Recall that, by convention, we take
v_{j}^{r}(0)=0
for
r>0
and
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
.

Proposition 7.5

(Proof in Section
9.1
)
.

Let
\delta
be as in Condition
1
. There exists
R<\infty
and for any
c<\delta
a corresponding
K(c)<\infty
such that for any
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
,
y^{r}\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
and
t\geq 0
,
we have

\sup_{r\geq R}E_{y^{r}}\left[e^{cv^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,S}_{j}(t))}\right]<K(c).

The next two results are proved in Section
10.3
. Recall the constant
\lambda
from Section
2.4
.

Proposition 7.6

(Proof in Section
10.3
)
.

There exists a constant
B_{\hat{h}}\in(0,\infty)
and
R\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
q^{1},q^{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{J}_{+}
and
r\geq R
, we have

|\lambda|(\tilde{d}(q^{2})-\tilde{d}(q^{1}))\leq h\cdot\left(q^{2}-q^{1}\right)+B_{\hat{h}}|KM^{r}q^{2}-KM^{r}q^{1}|_{2}.

Proposition 7.7

(Proof in Section
10.3
)
.

There exists a constant
B_{\tilde{d}}<\infty
such that for all
q^{1},q^{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{J}_{+}
we have

|\tilde{d}(q^{1})-\tilde{d}(q^{2})|\leq B_{\tilde{d}}|q^{1}-q^{2}|_{2}

We now proceed to the proof of Proposition
3.5
.

Proof of Proposition
3.5
.

By adding and subtracting
\hat{h}(KM\hat{Q}^{r})
, and using Proposition
2.1
, Proposition
3.4
, and the Lipschitz property of the map
\hat{h}
, it is sufficient to show that for any
\epsilon_{0}\in(0,1)
and
M<\infty
there exists
T^{*},R\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
r\geq R
,
T\geq T^{*}
,
y^{r}\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
satisfying
\hat{q}^{r}\leq M
and
\hat{\Upsilon}^{r}\leq r^{-1}M
, and
t\geq 0
we have

E_{y^{r}}\left[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(t+s)\right)ds\right]\leq\epsilon_{0},\;\;E_{y^{r}}\left[\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\varsigma s}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(t+s)\right)ds\right]\leq\epsilon_{0}.

We now fix
\epsilon_{0}\in(0,1)
and let
\epsilon>0
be arbitrary which will be chosen suitably later depending on
\epsilon_{0}
.

The main idea behind the proof is that, on average, the vectors
v^{c}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(t))
reduce
\tilde{d}(\hat{Q}^{r}(t))
faster than other factors like randomness and the vectors
v^{b}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(t))
(which are used to keep the queues nonempty) increase
\tilde{d}(\hat{Q}^{r}(t))
. This is used to show that for a given
\epsilon>0
there exists
k<\infty
such that for all sufficiently large
r
and all
t\geq 0
we have

E_{y^{r}}\left[\int_{t}^{t+kr^{-1}}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)ds\right]\leq\epsilon 3kr^{-1},

(43)

and from this the proposition follows readily.
Fix
\epsilon\in(0,1)
and
M<\infty
.
Proposition
3.4
implies that there exist constants
B_{1}<\infty
and
c>0
such that for sufficiently large
r
, all
t\geq 0
, and any
y^{r}\in\mathcal{Y}^{r}
satisfying
\hat{q}^{r}\leq M
and
\hat{\Upsilon}^{r}\leq r^{-1}M
we have

E_{y^{r}}\left[e^{c|\hat{W}^{r}(t)|}\right]\leq B_{1}
.
From the definition of
\tilde{d}
there exists a constant
B_{2}>0
such that for all
q\in\mathbb{R}^{J}_{+}
and
r
we have

\tilde{d}(q)\leq B_{2}e^{\frac{c}{2}|KM^{r}q|}

and

\tilde{d}(q)\leq B_{2}|KM^{r}q|
.
For notational convenience let
\xi=\frac{\epsilon}{\left(1+4|\lambda||\tilde{\lambda}|^{-1}\right)B_{1}B_{2}}\wedge 1
, where
\tilde{\lambda}
was defined in (
5
), and define for
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{r,n,t,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\sup_{0\leq s\leq rn}|A_{j}^{r,t}(s)-s\alpha^{r}_{j}|\geq\frac{1}{5}nr^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\cup\left\{\sup_{0\leq s\leq r\max_{i}\{C_{i}\}n}|S_{j}^{r,t}(s)-s\beta^{r}_{j}|\geq\frac{1}{5}nr^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\cup\left\{r\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\geq\xi n\right\}\cup\left\{\beta^{r}_{j}r^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\geq\frac{1}{5}\xi n\right\},

\mathcal{A}^{r,n,t}=\cup_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{A}^{r,n,t,j},

and

\mathcal{B}^{r,n,t}\doteq\mathcal{A}^{r,n,t}\cup\left\{\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi nr^{-1})\right)>\xi n\right\}\cup\Big\{\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}t+rn}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\geq\xi nr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa}\Big\}.

Main proof idea.
We will choose
k
large enough that
P(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t})
is small and we will show that on
(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t})^{c}
, because
\hat{\Upsilon}^{r}(t)
has a small impact after
t+\xi kr^{-1}
,
\hat{Q}^{r}(s)
has favorable behavior for
s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
. When proving the key estimate (
43
) we break up the interval
[t,t+kr^{-1}]
into the subintervals
[t,t+\xi k(1+4|\lambda||\tilde{\lambda}|^{-1})]
and
[t+\xi k(1+4|\lambda||\tilde{\lambda}|^{-1}),t+kr^{-1}]
. We can think of
\xi
as determining the size of the first subinterval over which we rely on a crude bound on
E[\tilde{d}(\hat{Q}^{r}(s))]
based on Proposition
3.4
, but because
\xi
is small (recall its definition in terms of
\epsilon
) we can use the length of the subinterval to show that

E_{y^{r}}\left[\int_{t}^{t+\xi k(1+4|\lambda||\tilde{\lambda}|^{-1})}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)ds\right]\leq\epsilon kr^{-1}.

For the second subinterval we demonstrate that on the set
(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t})^{c}

\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)
is small for all
s\in[t+\xi k(1+4|\lambda||\tilde{\lambda}|^{-1}),t+kr^{-1}]
, which combined with the fact that
(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t})^{c}
occurs with high probability, allows us to show

E_{y^{r}}\left[\int_{t+\xi k(1+4|\lambda||\tilde{\lambda}|^{-1})}^{t+kr^{-1}}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)ds\right]\leq 2\epsilon kr^{-1}

and complete the proof of (
43
) from which the Proposition follows easily by appealing to Proposition
2.1
.

Note that for any
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
we have

\displaystyle r\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\leq\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)=0\}}\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+r^{-1}\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0\}}u^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t))

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)=0\}}r^{-1}M+r^{-1}\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0\}}u^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t))

and, similarly

\beta^{r}_{j}r^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\leq\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,S}_{j}(t)=0\}}\beta^{r}_{j}r^{-\frac{1}{2}}M+\beta^{r}_{j}r^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,S}_{j}(t)>0\}}v^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,S}_{j}(t)).

This (recall that
\sup_{j}\beta^{r}_{j}<\infty
), combined with Propositions
6.2
((
14
), (
15
), with
c_{1}=1/2
,
c_{2}=0
),
7.5
, and
6.1
implies that there exist constants
\tilde{B}_{1},\tilde{B}_{2}<\infty

such that for all
t\geq 0
and all sufficiently large
r
we have

P_{y^{r}}\left(\mathcal{A}^{r,n,t}\right)\leq\tilde{B}_{1}e^{-n\tilde{B}_{2}}
.
In addition, for
r\geq\frac{1}{\xi}
we have

\displaystyle P_{y^{r}}\left(\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}t+rn}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\geq\xi nr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa}\right)\leq P_{y^{r}}\left(\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}(t+\xi n)}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\geq\xi nr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{J}P_{y^{r}}\left(\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}(t+\xi n)}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\geq r\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)+\frac{\xi n}{2J}r^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa}\right)+\sum_{j=1}^{J}P_{y^{r}}\left(r\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\geq\frac{n\xi}{2J}r^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa}\right).

This, combined with Proposition
3.3
, Proposition
6.1
, and the fact that for any
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}

\displaystyle r\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)=0\}}r\hat{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}+\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0\}}u^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)=0\}}M+\mathcal{I}_{\{\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t)>0\}}u^{r}_{j}(\tau^{r,A}_{j}(t))

implies that there exist constant
\tilde{B}_{3},\tilde{B}_{4}<\infty
such that for sufficiently large
r
and for all
t\geq 0
and
n\geq 0
we have

P_{y^{r}}\left(\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}t+rn}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\geq\xi nr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa}\right)\leq\tilde{B}_{3}e^{-n\tilde{B}_{4}}.

Furthermore, since
\tilde{d}(\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi nr^{-1}))\leq B_{2}|\hat{W}^{r}(t+\xi nr^{-1})|
and
E_{y^{r}}\left[e^{c|\hat{W}^{r}(t+\xi nr^{-1})|}\right]\leq B_{1}
for sufficiently large
r
we have

P_{y^{r}}\left(\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi nr^{-1})\right)>\xi n\right)\leq P_{y^{r}}\left(|\hat{W}^{r}(t+\xi nr^{-1})|>\frac{\xi n}{B_{2}}\right)\leq B_{1}e^{-\frac{c\xi}{B_{2}}n}.

Consequently we know there exist constants
B_{3},B_{4}<\infty
such that for sufficiently large
r
we have for all
t\geq 0
and
n\geq 0

P_{y^{r}}\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,n,t}\right)\leq B_{3}e^{-nB_{4}}
.

Our goal is to show that for any
\epsilon>0
we can choose corresponding
k,R<\infty
sufficiently large such that for all
r\geq R
(
43
) holds. Our choices of
k,R<\infty
will have to satisfy a large number of inequalities used throughout the remainder of the proof, so for the ease of the reader we list them all in one place below and reference them later when needed.

Summary 1
.

Recall the definitions of
\mathcal{B}^{r,n,t}
,
B_{1}
,
B_{2}
,
B_{3}
,
B_{4}
,
\xi
, and
c
. We choose the constants
k\geq\frac{1}{\xi}
and
R<\infty
such that,
B^{\frac{1}{2}}_{3}e^{-k\frac{B_{4}}{2}}B_{2}B^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}\leq\epsilon
and, for all
r\geq R
, the following hold:

(a)

For all
t\geq 0
we have
E_{y^{r}}\left[e^{c|\hat{W}^{r}(t)|}\right]\leq B_{1}

(b)

For all
q\in\mathbb{R}^{J}_{+}
, we have
\tilde{d}(q)\leq B_{2}e^{\frac{c}{2}|KM^{r}q|}

and
\tilde{d}(q)\leq B_{2}|KM^{r}q|

(c)

For all
t\geq 0
we have
P_{y^{r}}\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)\leq B_{3}e^{-kB_{4}}
.

(d)

\epsilon r^{\frac{1}{8}}\geq k
,
r\geq\frac{1}{\xi}
,
r\geq 25
, and
\epsilon r^{\frac{1}{16}}\geq\xi k
.

(e)

2|\theta|+|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\leq r^{\frac{1}{8}}

(f)

For all
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
we have
r\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\rho^{r}_{j}-C_{i}\right)\geq 2\theta_{i}

(g)

J(c_{2}+1)r^{\kappa-1}+C_{i}\epsilon r^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-\frac{15}{16}}+|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

(h)

For all
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
we have
\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\rho^{r}_{j}-C_{i}\right)<0

(i)

|h||\beta^{r}||\rho-\rho^{r}|+|h||\beta^{r}-\beta|\left(\max_{z\in\mathcal{M}}|v^{c}(z)|+\max_{z\in\left\{0,1\right\}^{J}}|v^{b}(z)|\right)\leq\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{4}

(j)

|\alpha^{r}|\leq 2|\alpha|

(k)

\left(\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}+2|h||\alpha|\right)\epsilon r^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-\frac{15}{16}}+|h|\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

(l)

\frac{\epsilon}{|\lambda|}r^{-\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{B_{\hat{h}}}{|\lambda|}I^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}\leq\xi k

(m)

B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+J^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{\tilde{d}}r^{-1}+\frac{\epsilon}{|\lambda|}r^{-\frac{1}{4}}+2B_{\hat{h}}I^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\epsilon}{|\lambda|}r^{-\frac{1}{4}}\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{8}}
.

We now bound
\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(\cdot)\right)
from above on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
.
Using (
9
),
on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
r\geq R
, any
t+\xi kr^{-1}\leq s_{1}<s_{2}\leq t+kr^{-1}
, and any
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\mathcal{I}_{\{s_{2}-t\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)>s_{1}-t\}}-\frac{1}{r}\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)>\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)\}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r}A_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(s_{2}-t-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right)-\frac{1}{r}A_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(s_{1}-t-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{r}S_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right)+\frac{1}{r}S_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}A_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(s_{2}-t-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\right)\right)-\frac{1}{r}A_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(s_{1}-t-\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{r}S_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right)+\frac{1}{r}S_{j}^{r,t}\left(r^{2}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right),

since on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
we have
\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\leq\xi kr^{-1}
meaning
\mathcal{I}_{\{s_{2}-t\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)>s_{1}-t\}}=0
,
and so

\displaystyle\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha^{r}_{j}r\left(s_{2}-s_{1}\right)+\frac{4}{5}kr^{-\frac{1}{2}}-\beta^{r}_{j}r\left(\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha^{r}_{j}r\left(s_{2}-s_{1}\right)-\beta^{r}_{j}r\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})\right)+\beta^{r}_{j}r\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)+\frac{4}{5}kr^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha^{r}_{j}r\left(s_{2}-s_{1}\right)-\beta^{r}_{j}r\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})\right)+kr^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha^{r}_{j}r\left(s_{2}-s_{1}\right)-\beta^{r}_{j}r\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})\right)+\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

due to Summary
1
part (d) and the fact that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
we have
\beta^{r}_{j}r^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\leq\frac{1}{5}\xi k\leq\frac{1}{5}k
.

A similar calculation, using the inequalities,

\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}-\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)-\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)\right)^{+}\leq\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1}),

-\frac{1}{r}\mathcal{I}_{\{\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)\geq\bar{\Upsilon}^{S,r}_{j}(t)>\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t)\}}\geq-\frac{1}{r}
, properties in Summary
1
part (d), and the fact that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
we have
\bar{\Upsilon}^{A,r}_{j}(t)\leq\xi kr^{-1}
shows that
on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for any
t+\xi kr^{-1}\leq s_{1}<s_{2}\leq t+kr^{-1}
,
r\geq R
, and
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
we have

\displaystyle\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\alpha^{r}_{j}r\left(s_{2}-s_{1}\right)-\beta^{r}_{j}r\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})\right)-\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}.

Consequently on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for any
t+\xi kr^{-1}\leq s_{1}<s_{2}\leq t+kr^{-1}
,
r\geq R
, and
j\in\mathbb{A}_{J}
we have

|\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\hat{Q}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})-r\left(\alpha^{r}_{j}(s_{2}-s_{1})-\beta^{r}_{j}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{2})-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s_{1})\right)\right)|\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}.

(44)

We will next use Proposition
7.6
to bound
\tilde{d}(\hat{Q}^{r}(\cdot))
from above on the interval
[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
. For this we will need to bound

\sup_{s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]}|\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})|

for all
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
. Let
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
be arbitrary. We will first bound

\inf_{s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)
from below.
On the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
r\geq R
and
s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
we have from (
44
)

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})-|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle+r\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\frac{1}{\beta^{r}_{j}}\left(\alpha^{r}_{j}(s-t-\xi kr^{-1})-\beta^{r}_{j}\left(\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}_{j}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+r(s-t-\xi kr^{-1})\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\rho^{r}_{j}-C_{i}\right)-|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+2\theta_{i}(s-t-\xi kr^{-1})-|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+2\theta_{i}kr^{-1}-|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+2\theta_{i}\epsilon r^{-\frac{7}{8}}-|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})-\left(2|\theta_{i}|+|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\right)\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}\geq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})-\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

due to equation (
44
) and Summary
1
parts (d), (e), and (f). Therefore on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
r\geq R
we have

\inf_{s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)\geq-\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}.

Now we bound
\sup_{s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]}\left(\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)
from above.
For all
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
define

\gamma^{r,i}_{0}\doteq\inf\left\{s\geq t+\xi kr^{-1}:\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)\geq Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\right\}

and for
l\geq 0
define

\gamma^{r,i}_{2l+1}\doteq\inf\left\{s\geq\gamma^{r,i}_{2l}:\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)<Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\right\},\;\;\gamma^{r,i}_{2l+2}\doteq\inf\left\{s\geq\gamma^{r,i}_{2l+1}:\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)\geq Jc_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\right\}.

Using Proposition
2.6
part (d) and (
44
), for
r\geq R
, on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
, for any
l\geq 0
and all

s\in[\gamma^{r,i}_{2l},\gamma^{r,i}_{2l+1})\cap[\gamma^{r,i}_{2l},t+kr^{-1}]
,

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)

\displaystyle\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(\gamma^{r,i}_{2l})+r\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\frac{1}{\beta^{r}_{j}}\left(\alpha^{r}_{j}(s-\gamma^{r,i}_{2l})-\beta^{r}_{j}\left(\bar{B}^{r}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}(\gamma^{r,i}_{2l})\right)\right)+|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+J(c_{2}+1)r^{\kappa-1}+r(s-\gamma^{r,i}_{2l})\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\rho^{r}_{j}-C_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+r^{-1}C_{i}\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}t+rk}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(u)=1\}}du+|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+J(c_{2}+1)r^{\kappa-1}+r(s-\gamma^{r,i}_{2l})\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\rho^{r}_{j}-C_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{i}\xi kr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-1}+|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+J(c_{2}+1)r^{\kappa-1}+r(s-\gamma^{r,i}_{2l})\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\rho^{r}_{j}-C_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{i}\epsilon r^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-\frac{15}{16}}+|\frac{1}{\beta^{r}}|_{1}\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+r(s-\gamma^{r,i}_{2l})\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}K_{i,j}\rho^{r}_{j}-C_{i}\right)+\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

due to Summary
1
parts (d), (g) and (h), and the fact that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
we have
r^{-1}\int_{r^{2}t}^{r^{2}t+rk}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{I}_{\{\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=1\}}ds\leq\xi kr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-1}
.
In addition, for
r\geq R
on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
, for all
s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},\gamma^{r,i}_{0})\cap[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
and
s\in[\gamma^{r,i}_{2l+1},\gamma^{r,i}_{2l+2})\cap[\gamma^{r,i}_{2l+1},t+kr^{-1}]
for any
l\geq 0
we have

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle J(c_{2}+1)r^{\kappa-1}\leq J(c_{2}+1)r^{\kappa-1}+\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\leq\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})+\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

due to Summary
1
part (g). Since
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
was arbitrary we have shown that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
r\geq R
and
i\in\mathbb{A}_{I}
we have

\sup_{s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]}|\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}_{i}(t+\xi kr^{-1})|\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}.

(45)

Now we will apply Proposition
7.6
to bound
\tilde{d}(\hat{Q}^{r}(\cdot))
from above on the interval
[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
.
From Proposition
7.7
,
for any
q\in\mathbb{R}^{J}_{+}
,

|\tilde{d}(q)-\tilde{d}(\left(q-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right)\vee 0)|\leq B_{\tilde{d}}|q-\left(q-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right)\vee 0|_{2}\leq B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}.

(46)

Define

\zeta^{r}_{0}\doteq\inf\left\{s\geq t+\xi kr^{-1}:\tilde{d}\left(Q^{r}(r^{2}s)\right)\leq B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right\}

and for
l\geq 0
define

\displaystyle\zeta^{r}_{2l+1}

\displaystyle\doteq\inf\left\{s\geq\zeta^{r}_{2l}:\tilde{d}\left(Q^{r}(r^{2}s)\right)>B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right\},

\displaystyle\zeta^{r}_{2l+2}

\displaystyle\doteq\inf\left\{s\geq\zeta^{r}_{2l+1}:\tilde{d}\left(Q^{r}(r^{2}s)\right)\leq B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right\}.

Note that if
\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s)\not\in\mathcal{M}
then due to Lemma
7.4
we have
\tilde{d}\left(\left(Q^{r}(r^{2}s)-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right)\vee 0\right)=0
so, from (
46
),

\displaystyle\tilde{d}\left(Q^{r}(r^{2}s)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{d}\left(\left(Q^{r}(r^{2}s)-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right)\vee 0\right)+|\tilde{d}(Q^{r}(r^{2}s))-\tilde{d}(\left(Q^{r}(r^{2}s)-\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}\right)\vee 0)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa}.

Consequently if
\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)>B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}
then
\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s)\in\mathcal{M}
(here we have used the fact that for any
r>0
and
q\in\mathbb{R}^{J}_{+}
we have
\tilde{d}(\frac{1}{r}q)=\frac{1}{r}\tilde{d}(q)
). In particular,
\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s)\in\mathcal{M}
for all
s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},\zeta^{r}_{0})\cap[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
and all
s\in[\zeta^{r}_{2l+1},\zeta^{r}_{2l+2})\cap[\zeta^{r}_{2l+1},t+kr^{-1}]
for any
l\geq 0
.

Next note that from Definition
2.3
for all
r\geq R
if
\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s)\in\mathcal{M}
and
\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(s)=0
we have

\mathfrak{b}^{r}(s)=\rho-v^{c}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))-v^{b}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))

and so

\displaystyle h\cdot\left(\alpha^{r}-\beta^{r}\mathfrak{b}^{r}(s)\right)=h\cdot\left(\alpha^{r}-\beta^{r}\left(\rho-v^{c}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))-v^{b}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))\right)\right)

\displaystyle=h\cdot\left(-\beta^{r}\left(\rho-\rho^{r}\right)+\beta v^{c}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))+\beta v^{b}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+(\beta^{r}-\beta)v^{c}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))+(\beta^{r}-\beta)v^{b}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))\right)

\displaystyle\leq|h||\beta^{r}||\rho-\rho^{r}|+|h||\beta^{r}-\beta|\left(\max_{z\in\mathcal{M}}|v^{c}(z)|+\max_{z\in\left\{0,1\right\}^{J}}|v^{b}(z)|\right)

\displaystyle\quad+h\beta\cdot v^{c}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))+h\beta\cdot v^{b}(\mathcal{Z}^{r}(s))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{4}-|\tilde{\lambda}|+|h||\beta|\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{4|h|\beta|}\leq-\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}

(47)

where we have used Summary
1
part (i) and the fact that due to Definition
2.3
we have
\max_{z\in\mathcal{Z}}\left\{h\beta\cdot v^{c}(z)\right\}\leq-|\tilde{\lambda}|
and
\max_{z\in\left\{0,1\right\}}|v^{b}(z)|\leq\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{4|\beta||h|}
.
Next, on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
r\geq R
and
s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},\zeta^{r}_{0})\cap[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
we have from (
44
)

\displaystyle h\cdot\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)-|h|\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq rh\cdot\left(\alpha^{r}\left(s-t-\xi kr^{-1}\right)-\beta^{r}\left(\bar{B}^{r}(s)-\bar{B}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{t+\xi kr^{-1}}^{s}\mathcal{I}_{\{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}z)=0\}}rh\cdot\left(\alpha^{r}-\beta^{r}\mathfrak{b}^{r}(r^{2}z)\right)dz

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{t+\xi kr^{-1}}^{s}\mathcal{I}_{\{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}z)>0\}}rh\cdot\left(\alpha^{r}-\beta^{r}\mathfrak{b}^{r}(r^{2}z)\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq-r\left(s-t-\xi kr^{-1}-\int_{t+\xi kr^{-1}}^{s}\mathcal{I}_{\{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}z)>0\}}dz\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}

\displaystyle\quad+r(h\cdot\alpha^{r})\int_{t+\xi kr^{-1}}^{s}\mathcal{I}_{\{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}z)>0\}}dz

where the last line is from (
47
). Thus

\displaystyle h\cdot\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq-r\left(s-t-\xi kr^{-1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}+\left(\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}+2|h||\alpha|\right)\xi kr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-1}+|h|\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq-r\left(s-t-\xi kr^{-1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}+\left(\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}+2|h||\alpha|\right)\epsilon r^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-\frac{15}{16}}+|h|\epsilon r^{-\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq-r\left(s-t-\xi kr^{-1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}+\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

(48)

due to Summary
1
parts (d), (j), and (k), and the fact that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
we have

\int_{t}^{t+kr^{-1}}\mathcal{I}_{\{\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{r}(r^{2}z)>0\}}dz\leq\xi kr^{\frac{7}{4}\kappa-2}.
An almost identical argument shows that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
r\geq R
, any
l\geq 0
, and all
s\in[\zeta^{r}_{2l+1},\zeta^{r}_{2l+2})\cap[\zeta^{r}_{2l+1},t+kr^{-1}]
we have

h\cdot\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\hat{Q}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})\right)\leq-r\left(s-\zeta^{r}_{2l+1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2}+\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}.

(49)

From Proposition
7.6
,
for
r\geq R
, on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
s\in[t+\xi kr^{-1},\zeta^{r}_{0})\cap[t+\xi kr^{-1},t+kr^{-1}]
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)+\frac{1}{|\lambda|}h\cdot\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{B_{\hat{h}}}{|\lambda|}|\hat{W}^{r}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\xi k-r\left(s-t-\xi kr^{-1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2|\lambda|}+\frac{\epsilon}{|\lambda|}r^{-\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{B_{\hat{h}}}{|\lambda|}I^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\xi k-r\left(s-t-\xi kr^{-1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2|\lambda|}

where we have used (
48
), (
45
),
Summary
1
part (l), and the fact that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
we have
\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})\right)\leq\xi k
. This implies that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for
r\geq R
if
\zeta^{r}_{0}-t-\xi kr^{-1}>\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}r^{-1}
we have

\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}\left(t+\xi kr^{-1}+\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}r^{-1}\right)\right)\leq 2\xi k-r\left(\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}r^{-1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2|\lambda|}\leq 0

which contradicts the definition of
\zeta^{r}_{0}
. Consequently, on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for
r\geq R
we have

\zeta^{r}_{0}\leq t+\xi kr^{-1}+\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}r^{-1}.

(50)

Similarly, for
r\geq R
on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for all
s\in[\zeta^{r}_{2l+1},\zeta^{r}_{2l+2})\cap[\zeta^{r}_{2l+1},t+kr^{-1}]
for any
l\geq 0
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})\right)+\frac{1}{|\lambda|}h\cdot\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\hat{Q}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})\right)+\frac{B_{\hat{h}}}{|\lambda|}|\hat{W}^{r}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})\right)+\frac{1}{|\lambda|}h\cdot\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)-\hat{Q}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{B_{\hat{h}}}{|\lambda|}|\hat{W}^{r}(s)-\hat{W}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})|_{2}+\frac{B_{\hat{h}}}{|\lambda|}|\hat{W}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})-\hat{W}^{r}(t+\xi kr^{-1})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+J^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{\tilde{d}}r^{-1}-r\left(s-\zeta^{r}_{2l+1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2|\lambda|}+\frac{\epsilon}{|\lambda|}r^{-\frac{1}{4}}+2B_{\hat{h}}I^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\epsilon}{|\lambda|}r^{-\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{8}}-r\left(s-\zeta^{r,\epsilon}_{2l+1}\right)\frac{|\tilde{\lambda}|}{2|\lambda|}

where we have used (
45
), (
49
),
Summary
1
part (m), and the fact that by the definition of
\zeta^{r}_{2l+1}
and Proposition
7.7
we have
\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})\right)\leq B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}+J^{\frac{1}{2}}B_{\tilde{d}}r^{-1}
.
This, combined with the fact that for
r\geq R
, any
l\geq 0
, and all
s\in[\zeta^{r}_{2l},\zeta^{r}_{2l+1})
we have
\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)\leq B_{\tilde{d}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{c}_{2}r^{\kappa-1}\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{8}}
due to the definition of
\zeta^{r}_{2l+1}
and Summary
1
part (m), implies that on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for
r\geq R
and all
s\in[\zeta^{r}_{0},t+kr^{-1}]
we have

\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{8}}
.
Therefore, from (
50
), on the set
\left(\mathcal{B}^{r,k,t}\right)^{c}
for
r\geq R
we have

\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)\leq\epsilon r^{-\frac{1}{8}}\mbox{ for all }s\in[t+(\xi k+4\xi k|\lambda||\tilde{\lambda}|^{-1})r^{-1},t+kr^{-1}].

(51)

Note that for
r\geq R
we have

\displaystyle E_{y^{r}}\int_{t}^{t+kr^{-1}}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)ds

\displaystyle\leq E_{y^{r}}\int_{t}^{t+\left(\xi k+\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}\right)r^{-1}}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)ds

\displaystyle+E_{y^{r}}\int_{t+\left(\xi k+\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}\right)r^{-1}}^{t+kr^{-1}}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)ds.

(52)

and

\displaystyle E_{y^{r}}\int_{t}^{t+\left(\xi k+\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}\right)r^{-1}}\tilde{d}\left(\hat{Q}^{r}(s)\right)ds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{t}^{t+\left(\xi k+\frac{4\xi k|\lambda|}{|\tilde{\lambda}|}\right)r^{-1}}B_{2}E_{y^{r}}\left[e^{c|\hat{W}^{r}(s)|}\right]ds

(53)
